self._make_pointwise_result(self._call(text))\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nJUDGE_MAP = {\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"gpt-4o\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": (OpenAIJudge, {\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"model_name\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"gpt-4o\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"}),\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"gpt-5.5\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": (OpenAIJudge, {\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"model_name\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"claude-opus-4-7-20250219\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"}),\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"claude-sonnet-4.6\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": (AnthropicJudge, {\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"model_name\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"claude-sonnet-4-6-20250514\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"}),\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"gemini-2.5-pro\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": (GoogleJudge, {\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"model_name\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"gemini-2.5-pro\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"}),\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"deepseek-v4-pro\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": (DeepSeekJudge, {\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"model_name\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"deepseek-v4-pro\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"}),\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"qwen3-235b\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": (OpenRouterJudge, {\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"model_name\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"qwen/qwen3-235b-a22b\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"}),\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"llama-3.3-70b\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": (VLLMJudge, {\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"model_name\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"meta-llama/Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"}),\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"qwen2.5-72b\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": (VLLMJudge, {\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"model_name\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Qwen/Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"}),\\\\\\\\n}\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\ndef get_judge(judge_name: str, **kwargs) -> JudgeInterface:\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Factory function to get judge by name.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n    if judge_name not in JUDGE_MAP:\\\\\\\\n        raise ValueError(f\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Unknown judge: {judge_name}. Available: {list(JUDGE_MAP.keys())}\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\")\\\\\\\\n    cls, defaults = JUDGE_MAP[judge_name]\\\\\\\\n    params = {**defaults, **kwargs}\\\\\\\\n    return cls(**params)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n# Append mistral-large-2 to JUDGE_MAP\\\\\\\\nJUDGE_MAP[\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"mistral-large-2\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"] = (VLLMJudge, {\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"model_name\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"mistralai/Mistral-Large-Instruct-2407\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"})\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"stderr\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"return_code\\\\\\\": 0}, \\\\\\\"error\\\\\\\": null, \\\\\\\"metadata\\\\\\\": {\\\\\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\\\\\": 0.51}}\\\"}\",\"structuredContent\":{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Unified LLM judge interface for pointwise and pairwise evaluation.\\\\n\\\\nEach judge wraps an API (OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, DeepSeek, OpenRouter, vLLM)\\\\nand returns a standardized score dict. Supports both MT-Bench-style pointwise\\\\nscoring and Arena-Hard-style pairwise comparison.\\\\n\\\\nEnv vars: OPENAI_API_KEY, ANTHROPIC_API_KEY, GOOGLE_API_KEY,\\\\n          DEEPSEEK_API_KEY, OPENROUTER_API_KEY\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nimport os\\\\nimport re\\\\nfrom abc import ABC, abstractmethod\\\\n\\\\nPOINTWISE_JUDGE_PROMPT = \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\nPlease act as an impartial judge and evaluate the quality of the response provided by an AI assistant to the user question displayed below. Rate the response on a scale of 1 to 10, where 1 is the worst and 10 is the best. Consider factors such as helpfulness, relevance, accuracy, depth, creativity, and level of detail. Begin your evaluation by providing a short explanation. Be as objective as possible. After providing your explanation, output your rating strictly following this format: \\\\\\\"[[rating]]\\\\\\\", for example: \\\\\\\"[[5]]\\\\\\\".\\\\n\\\\n[Question]\\\\n{prompt}\\\\n\\\\n[The Start of Assistant's Answer]\\\\n{response}\\\\n[The End of Assistant's Answer]\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nPAIRWISE_JUDGE_PROMPT = \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\nPlease act as an impartial judge and evaluate the quality of the responses \\\\\\\\\\\\nprovided by two AI assistants to the user question displayed below. You should \\\\\\\\\\\\nchoose the assistant that follows the user's instructions and answers the \\\\\\\\\\\\nuser's question better. Your evaluation should consider factors such as the \\\\\\\\\\\\nhelpfulness, relevance, accuracy, depth, creativity, and level of detail of \\\\\\\\\\\\ntheir responses. Begin your evaluation by comparing the two responses and \\\\\\\\\\\\nprovide a short explanation. Avoid any position biases and ensure that the \\\\\\\\\\\\norder in which the responses were presented does not influence your decision. \\\\\\\\\\\\nDo not allow the length of the responses to influence your evaluation. Do not \\\\\\\\\\\\nfavor certain names of the assistants. Be as objective as possible. After \\\\\\\\\\\\nproviding your explanation, output your final verdict by strictly following \\\\\\\\\\\\nthis format: \\\\\\\"[[A]]\\\\\\\" if assistant A is better, \\\\\\\"[[B]]\\\\\\\" if assistant B is \\\\\\\\\\\\nbetter, and \\\\\\\"[[C]]\\\\\\\" for a tie.\\\\n\\\\n[User Question]\\\\n{prompt}\\\\n\\\\n[The Start of Assistant A's Answer]\\\\n{response_a}\\\\n[The End of Assistant A's Answer]\\\\n\\\\n[The Start of Assistant B's Answer]\\\\n{response_b}\\\\n[The End of Assistant B's Answer]\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef _parse_verdict(raw: str) -> float | None:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Extract pairwise verdict. Returns 1.0/0.0/0.5/None.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    match = re.search(r\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\[\\\\\\\\[([ABC])\\\\\\\\]\\\\\\\\]\\\\\\\", raw)\\\\n    if not match:\\\\n        return None\\\\n    v = match.group(1)\\\\n    if v == \\\\\\\"A\\\\\\\":\\\\n        return 1.0\\\\n    elif v == \\\\\\\"B\\\\\\\":\\\\n        return 0.0\\\\n    return 0.5\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef _parse_pointwise_score(raw: str) -> float | None:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Extract pointwise rating [[N]] where N in 1-10. Returns float or None.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    match = re.search(r\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\[\\\\\\\\[(\\\\\\\\d+(?:\\\\\\\\.\\\\\\\\d+)?)\\\\\\\\]\\\\\\\\]\\\\\\\", raw)\\\\n    if not match:\\\\n        return None\\\\n    score = float(match.group(1))\\\\n    if 1 <= score <= 10:\\\\n        return score\\\\n    return None\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass JudgeInterface(ABC):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Base class for all LLM judges.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    @abstractmethod\\\\n    def evaluate(self, prompt: str, response_a: str, response_b: str,\\\\n                 swap_position: bool = False) -> dict:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Pairwise evaluation.\\\\n\\\\n        Returns:\\\\n            {score: float|None, raw_output: str, position: \\\\\\\"AB\\\\\\\"|\\\\\\\"BA\\\\\\\"}\\\\n            score: 1.0=A wins, 0.0=B wins, 0.5=tie, None=parse failure\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        pass\\\\n\\\\n    @abstractmethod\\\\n    def evaluate_pointwise(self, prompt: str, response: str) -> dict:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Pointwise evaluation (1-10 scale).\\\\n\\\\n        Returns:\\\\n            {score: float|None (1-10), raw_output: str}\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        pass\\\\n\\\\n    def _build_prompt(self, prompt: str, response_a: str, response_b: str,\\\\n                      swap_position: bool) -> tuple[str, str]:\\\\n        if swap_position:\\\\n            text = PAIRWISE_JUDGE_PROMPT.format(\\\\n                prompt=prompt, response_a=response_b, response_b=response_a)\\\\n            return text, \\\\\\\"BA\\\\\\\"\\\\n        else:\\\\n            text = PAIRWISE_JUDGE_PROMPT.format(\\\\n                \\\\n\\\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 3985 字符] ...\\\\n\\\\nm/v1\\\\\\\",\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n    def _call(self, text: str) -> str:\\\\n        resp = self.client.chat.completions.create(\\\\n            model=self.model_name,\\\\n            messages=[{\\\\\\\"role\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"user\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\": text}],\\\\n            temperature=0.0,\\\\n            max_tokens=1024,\\\\n        )\\\\n        return resp.choices[0].message.content\\\\n\\\\n    def evaluate(self, prompt, response_a, response_b, swap_position=False):\\\\n        text, position = self._build_prompt(prompt, response_a, response_b, swap_position)\\\\n        return self._make_result(self._call(text), position)\\\\n\\\\n    def evaluate_pointwise(self, prompt, response):\\\\n        text = self._build_pointwise_prompt(prompt, response)\\\\n        return self._make_pointwise_result(self._call(text))\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass OpenRouterJudge(JudgeInterface):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Judge using OpenRouter API (Qwen3-235B etc).\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self, model_name: str, api_key: [REDACTED] = None):\\\\n        from openai import OpenAI\\\\n        self.model_name = model_name\\\\n        self.client = OpenAI(\\\\n            api_key=[REDACTED] or os.environ[\\\\\\\"OPENROUTER_API_KEY\\\\\\\"],\\\\n            base_url=\\\\\\\"https://openrouter.ai/api/v1\\\\\\\",\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n    def _call(self, text: str) -> str:\\\\n        resp = self.client.chat.completions.create(\\\\n            model=self.model_name,\\\\n            messages=[{\\\\\\\"role\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"user\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\": text}],\\\\n            temperature=0.0,\\\\n            max_tokens=1024,\\\\n        )\\\\n        return resp.choices[0].message.content\\\\n\\\\n    def evaluate(self, prompt, response_a, response_b, swap_position=False):\\\\n        text, position = self._build_prompt(prompt, response_a, response_b, swap_position)\\\\n        return self._make_result(self._call(text), position)\\\\n\\\\n    def evaluate_pointwise(self, prompt, response):\\\\n        text = self._build_pointwise_prompt(prompt, response)\\\\n        return self._make_pointwise_result(self._call(text))\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass VLLMJudge(JudgeInterface):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Judge using local vLLM server.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self, model_name: str, base_url: str = \\\\\\\"http://localhost:8000\\\\\\\"):\\\\n        from openai import OpenAI\\\\n        self.model_name = model_name\\\\n        self.client = OpenAI(api_key=[REDACTED]"EMPTY\\\\\\\", base_url=f\\\\\\\"{base_url}/v1\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    def _call(self, text: str) -> str:\\\\n        resp = self.client.chat.completions.create(\\\\n            model=self.model_name,\\\\n            messages=[{\\\\\\\"role\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"user\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\": text}],\\\\n            temperature=0.0,\\\\n            max_tokens=1024,\\\\n        )\\\\n        return resp.choices[0].message.content\\\\n\\\\n    def evaluate(self, prompt, response_a, response_b, swap_position=False):\\\\n        text, position = self._build_prompt(prompt, response_a, response_b, swap_position)\\\\n        return self._make_result(self._call(text), position)\\\\n\\\\n    def evaluate_pointwise(self, prompt, response):\\\\n        text = self._build_pointwise_prompt(prompt, response)\\\\n        return self._make_pointwise_result(self._call(text))\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nJUDGE_MAP = {\\\\n    \\\\\\\"gpt-4o\\\\\\\": (OpenAIJudge, {\\\\\\\"model_name\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"gpt-4o\\\\\\\"}),\\\\n    \\\\\\\"gpt-5.5\\\\\\\": (OpenAIJudge, {\\\\\\\"model_name\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"gpt-5.5\\\\\\\"}),\\\\n    \\\\\\\"claude-opus-4.7\\\\\\\": (AnthropicJudge, {\\\\\\\"model_name\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"claude-opus-4-7-20250219\\\\\\\"}),\\\\n    \\\\\\\"claude-sonnet-4.6\\\\\\\": (AnthropicJudge, {\\\\\\\"model_name\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"claude-sonnet-4-6-20250514\\\\\\\"}),\\\\n    \\\\\\\"gemini-2.5-pro\\\\\\\": (GoogleJudge, {\\\\\\\"model_name\\\\\\\": (OpenRouterJudge, {\\\\\\\"model_name\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"qwen/qwen3-235b-a22b\\\\\\\"}),\\\\n    \\\\\\\"llama-3.3-70b\\\\\\\": (VLLMJudge, {\\\\\\\"model_name\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"meta-llama/Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct\\\\\\\"}),\\\\n    \\\\\\\"qwen2.5-72b\\\\\\\": (VLLMJudge, {\\\\\\\"model_name\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"Qwen/Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct\\\\\\\"}),\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef get_judge(judge_name: str, **kwargs) -> JudgeInterface:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Factory function to get judge by name.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    if judge_name not in JUDGE_MAP:\\\\n        raise ValueError(f\\\\\\\"Unknown judge: {judge_name}. Available: {list(JUDGE_MAP.keys())}\\\\\\\")\\\\n    cls, defaults = JUDGE_MAP[judge_name]\\\\n    params = {**defaults, **kwargs}\\\\n    return cls(**params)\\\\n\\\\n# Append mistral-large-2 to JUDGE_MAP\\\\nJUDGE_MAP[\\\\\\\"mistral-large-2\\\\\\\"] = (VLLMJudge, {\\\\\\\"model_name\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"mistralai/Mistral-Large-Instruct-2407\\\\\\\"})\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.51}}\"}}}\n{\"type\":\"assistant\",\"message\":{\"model\":\"claude-opus-4-6\",\"id\":\"msg_016CTs2AP6TYWTg59cg4XbEv\",\"type\":\"message\",\"role\":\"assistant\",\"content\":[{\"type\":\"thinking\",\"thinking\":\"Now I can see the `get_judge` function:\\n\\n```python\\ndef get_judge(judge_name: str, **kwargs) -> JudgeInterface:\\n    if judge_name not in JUDGE_MAP:\\n        raise ValueError(...)\\n    cls, defaults = JUDGE_MAP[judge_name]\\n    params = {**defaults, **kwargs}\\n    return cls(**params)\\n```\\n\\nThe key line is `params = {**defaults, **kwargs}`. This means kwargs from the config **override** defaults from JUDGE_MAP. \\n\\nSo when collect.py calls `get_judge(\\\"mistral-large-2\\\", model_name=\\\"mistral-large-instruct\\\", base_url=\\\"http://localhost:8003\\\")`:\\n\\n\\nThe defaults get overridden by the kwargs, so VLLMJudge receives `model_name=\\\"mistral-large-instruct\\\"` and `base_url=\\\"http://localhost:8003\\\"`, which it then passes to the OpenAI API as the model parameter.
